# About this item

*    No More Messy Oil Filter Changes: Orion Motor Tech's 10pc oil filter wrench set helps you remove and install oil filter caps quickly and easily with a 3/8" drive rachet wrench and 9 oil cap sockets in the most commonly needed sizes (24, 27, 29, 30, 32, 36, 38, 64, & 64.5 mm) 
*    Handle a Wide Range of Car Models: Our universal oil filter removal tool kit is compatible with 1.8, 2.5, & 5.7L Toyota engines, Mercedes, Cadillac, Buick, Saturn, SAAB, Dodge, Ford, Mini Cooper, and some BMW and Mack Truck models with canister oil filter housing and plastic cap; Not compatible with GMC, Hyundai. 
*    Low Profile Design for Tight Spaces: Engineered to access hard-to-reach areas with ease, our low profile oil filter socket set provides a perfect fit and maximum torque strength 
*    Quick Identification for Instant Use: Each 6 point oil filter cap wrench adapter is permanently marked with clear metric sizes, making it easy to select the right size for your specific vehicle 
*    Durable Build for Lasting Service: The CrV steel wrench and aluminum sockets are treated with electrocoating for unrivaled strength, endurance, and corrosion resistance to endure years of heavy use 

›[See more product details](https://www.amazon.com/dp/B0BJK5FSNR#productDetails)

[How customer reviews and ratings work](javascript:void(0))

Customer Reviews, including Product Star Ratings help customers to learn more about the product and decide whether it is the right product for them.

To calculate the overall star rating and percentage breakdown by star, we don’t use a simple average. Instead, our system considers things like how recent a review is and if the reviewer bought the item on Amazon. It also analyzed reviews to verify trustworthiness.

### Customers say

Customers find the oil filter wrench set to be of good quality and functionality, with one customer noting it works particularly well with cartridge-style filters. The set is compatible with Toyota Tacomas and includes everything needed. However, durability is a concern as Toyota-style sockets break with everyday use, and the material receives mixed feedback with some praising it while others find it to be cheap aluminum. Additionally, customers report issues with the grip, with one mentioning it doesn't hold on to ratchet or extension properly.

# Product Summary: OMT Oil Filter Wrench Set, 10Pc 3/8" Drive Low Profile Socket Removal Tool | 3/8" Drive Ratchet & 9 Cap Sockets (24 to 64.5mm), Low Profile 6-Point Design, Compatible With Toyota, VW, BMW, Mercedes

From Orion Motor Tech

4.4 out of 5 stars, 1,119 ratings

## About this Item

*   No More Messy Oil Filter Changes: Orion Motor Tech's 10pc oil filter wrench set helps you remove and install oil filter caps quickly and easily with a 3/8" drive rachet wrench and 9 oil cap sockets in the most commonly needed sizes (24, 27, 29, 30, 32, 36, 38, 64, & 64.5 mm)
*   Handle a Wide Range of Car Models: Our universal oil filter removal tool kit is compatible with 1.8, 2.5, & 5.7L Toyota engines, Mercedes, Cadillac, Buick, Saturn, SAAB, Dodge, Ford, Mini Cooper, and some BMW and Mack Truck models with canister oil filter housing and plastic cap; Not compatible with GMC, Hyundai.
*   Low Profile Design for Tight Spaces: Engineered to access hard-to-reach areas with ease, our low profile oil filter socket set provides a perfect fit and maximum torque strength
*   Quick Identification for Instant Use: Each 6 point oil filter cap wrench adapter is permanently marked with clear metric sizes, making it easy to select the right size for your specific vehicle
*   Durable Build for Lasting Service: The CrV steel wrench and aluminum sockets are treated with electrocoating for unrivaled strength, endurance, and corrosion resistance to endure years of heavy use

## Product Description

 Say goodbye to messy and frustrating oil filter changes with Orion Motor Tech's oil filter cap wrench set. Designed to make oil filter cap removal and installation quick and easy, this 10 piece oil filter wrench set includes a 3/8" drive ratchet wrench and 9 oil cap sockets in the most commonly needed sizes (24, 27, 29, 30, 32, 36, 38, 64, & 64.5 mm). Compatible with a wide range of car models, including 1.8, 2.5, & 5.7L Toyota engines, Mercedes, Cadillac, Buick, Saturn, SAAB, Dodge, Ford, Mini Cooper, and some BMW and Mack Truck models with canister oil filter housing and plastic cap, this universal oil filter removal tool kit is a must-have for any car enthusiast or mechanic. With its low profile design, precision machining, and quick identification markings, our oil filter socket set offers a perfect fit and maximum torque strength to easily access hard-to-reach areas. Made of high-quality materials with corrosion proof electrocoating, these heavy duty oil filter cap removal tools from Orion Motor Tech will ensure lasting dependable service.
